# November 5 — Pick photo objects directly on the preview

**0 new user-facing features; 1 usability improvement; 9 new pure geometry tests; 0 scaffolding additions.** Photo inspector → Composition objects → Pick object on photo arms a single-object picker. # November 5 — C054 text-color contrast review

**1 new source/browser-tested feature;0 separate fixes/scaffolding additions.** Drawing → Objects → Review text colors compares all visible text’s assigned color against an explicitly chosen opaque hexadecimal background. Source color alpha and object opacity are composited over that background before sRGB luminance calculation. Hidden/deleted text is excluded;locked text remains included. Stable text IDs/numbers lead to the existing nonmutating Find on canvas action. Object-list filters do not narrow this all-text review. Results/settings stay transient and do not edit projects/media or create Undo entries.

Uses the W3C [G18 formula](https://www.w3.org/WAI/WCAG22/Techniques/general/G18) and [unrounded threshold guidance](https://www.w3.org/WAI/WCAG22/Understanding/contrast-minimum.html),verified November5.4.5:1 is a normal-text color reference only;no final font/size or WCAG conformance claim. Near-threshold display cannot round a failing score up into a passing label. This tool does not sample actual pixels or infer backgrounds behind text,photos,shapes,patterns,groups or effects. UI keeps a short limitation visible and places the detailed explanation in a keyboard-operable disclosure. Users still review the finished output.
